Eine\nversandkostenfreie Lieferung wird f\u00fcr die Schweiz nicht angeboten.\n\n| Versandart | Laufzeit | Kosten |\n| --- | --- | --- |\n| Standard | 5\u20137 Werktage | CHF 14.90 |\n| Express | 3 Werktage | CHF 29.90 |\n| Sperrgut (Zubeh\u00f6rpakete ab 10 kg) | 7\u20139 Werktage | CHF 39.90 |\n\n# R\u00fcckgabe\n\nDie R\u00fcckgabefrist betr\u00e4gt f\u00fcr Privatkundinnen und Privatkunden in der Schweiz\n14 Tage ab Erhalt der Ware, abweichend von der allgemeinen Richtlinie. Die\nR\u00fccksendung erfolgt \u00fcber das im Kundenkonto hinterlegte Retourenportal; Sendungen\nohne Retourennummer k\u00f6nnen am Zoll nicht zugeordnet werden und werden\nzur\u00fcckgewiesen.\n\nErstattet wird der Warenwert einschliesslich der beim Kauf entrichteten\nEinfuhrabgaben. Die urspr\u00fcnglichen Versandkosten werden nicht erstattet.\n\n# Garantie\n\nDie Garantiebedingungen entsprechen der allgemeinen Richtlinie. Free\nshipping is not offered for the United Kingdom.\n\n| Service | Transit time | Cost |\n| --- | --- | --- |\n| Standard | 5\u20137 working days | \u00a312.90 |\n| Express | 3 working days | \u00a324.90 |\n| Bulky (accessory packs over 10 kg) | 7\u20139 working days | \u00a334.90 |\n\n# Returns\n\nConsumers in the United Kingdom have 14 days from delivery to return an order,\nwhich differs from the general policy. Returns must be booked through the returns\nportal in the account area: parcels arriving without a returns reference cannot be\nmatched to an order at the border and are refused.\n\nRefunds cover the goods and the import VAT paid at checkout. Original shipping\ncharges are not refunded.\n\n# Warranty\n\nWarranty terms follow the general policy. Claims are handled through a service\npartner in Reading, which typically adds three to five working days compared with\nclaims inside the European Union.\n\nOn-site repair is not offered in the United Kingdom.\n"

Standard delivery to the Netherlands and\nBelgium takes three to four working days; Luxembourg takes one working day longer\nbecause of the onward leg from the Belgian hub.\n\n| Destination | Standard | Express | Free over |\n| --- | --- | --- | --- |\n| Netherlands | 3\u20134 working days | 2 working days | \u20ac59 |\n| Belgium | 3\u20134 working days | 2 working days | \u20ac59 |\n| Luxembourg | 4\u20135 working days | 2 working days | \u20ac69 |\n\n# Installer collection\n\nInstaller partners in the Benelux may collect stock directly from the Antwerp\ndepot instead of waiting for delivery. Dutch and French translations\nof the installation guide are available on request but are not maintained at the\nsame revision as the English original; where the two differ, the English text is\nauthoritative.\n\n# Returns\n\nReturn windows and restocking fees follow the general policy. <details><summary>tool_use: Write — /home/sebi/workspace/sebi/chat-bot/corpus/content/release-notes-4-2-en.md</summary>

```json
{
  "file_path": "/home/sebi/workspace/sebi/chat-bot/corpus/content/release-notes-4-2-en.md",
  "content": "---\ntitle: Aurora Home \u2014 Release Notes 4.2\nsubtitle: Hub H2, Thermostat T3, Sensor S1 \u00b7 released 14 January 2026\nlang: en\nformat: html\nlayout: report\n---\n\n# Highlights\n\nRelease 4.2 concentrates on mesh stability in large properties and on making\nschedules easier to reason about. It is a staged rollout: hubs check nightly and\nupdate themselves, and connected devices follow over the following days.\n\n# New\n\n- Zone test now runs every zone in sequence from the app and reports which zones responded.\n- Holiday mode accepts a return date and clears itself automatically on that date.\n- Schedules can be copied between zones, including across properties on the same account.\n- The device list can be sorted by floor when a floor prefix is present in the name.\n\n# Changed\n\n- The minimum cycle time for heat-pump installations is now configurable between 5 and 30 minutes; the previous fixed value was 10 minutes.\n- Low-battery warnings fire at 2.6 V, raised from 2.5 V, to give more notice in cold rooms.\n- Pairing mode times out after 90 seconds instead of 60.\n\n# Fixed\n\n- A hub that lost its uplink during a firmware staging window could leave a device reporting an in-progress update indefinitely.\n- Renaming a zone no longer detaches its schedule.\n- Temperature offsets entered in Fahrenheit were stored without conversion on accounts created before 3.9.\n\n# Known issues\n\n- Sensors that have been offline for more than 30 days occasionally need to be re-paired rather than rejoining on their own.\n- The energy report understates consumption for the first partial month after installation.\n\n# Upgrade notes\n\nThere is no rollback. Properties with more than forty devices should expect the\nmesh to re-converge over several hours after the hub restarts; this is normal and\nneeds no intervention.\n"
}
```

  "content": "---\ntitle: Aurora Home \u2014 Versionshinweise 4.1\nsubtitle: Hub H2, Thermostat T3, Sensor S1 \u00b7 ver\u00f6ffentlicht am 6. Oktober 2025\nlang: de\nformat: pdf\nlayout: brief\n---\n\n# \u00dcberblick\n\nVersion 4.1 bringt die Modulation \u00fcber OpenTherm sowie mehrere Korrekturen an der\nZeitplanlogik. Die Verteilung erfolgt gestaffelt \u00fcber die n\u00e4chtliche Pr\u00fcfung des\nHubs.\n\n# Neu\n\n- Modulierender Betrieb \u00fcber OpenTherm f\u00fcr Kombi- und Brennwertger\u00e4te.\n- Korrekturwert f\u00fcr die Temperaturmessung je Thermostat in der App hinterlegbar.\n- Exportierbare Verbrauchs\u00fcbersicht im CSV-Format.\n\n# Ge\u00e4ndert\n\n- Die Zeitplanansicht zeigt bis zu sechs Schaltpunkte je Tag; zuvor waren es vier.\n- Der Frostschutz greift jetzt bei 7 \u00b0C statt bei 5 \u00b0C.\n- Die Reaktionszeit des Fensterkontakts wurde von zwei Sekunden auf unter eine Sekunde verk\u00fcrzt.\n\n# Behoben\n\n- Ein Zeitplan mit zwei Schaltpunkten zur selben Uhrzeit f\u00fchrte dazu, dass beide ignoriert wurden.\n- Der Urlaubsmodus liess sich nicht beenden, solange eine Automatisierung aktiv war.\n- Die App zeigte nach einem Hub-Neustart gelegentlich veraltete Messwerte an.\n\n# Hinweise zur Aktualisierung\n\nEin Zur\u00fccksetzen auf 4.0 ist nicht m\u00f6glich. Installationen mit\nSchnittstellenmodul AH-IM1 sollten nach der Aktualisierung einen Zonentest\ndurchf\u00fchren, da die Ansteuerung der Ventile neu kalibriert wird.\n"
}

  "content": "---\ntitle: Aurora Home \u2014 Release Notes 4.0\nsubtitle: Hub H2, Thermostat T3, Sensor S1 \u00b7 released 3 June 2025\nlang: en\nformat: docx\nlayout: manual\n---\n\n# Overview\n\nRelease 4.0 replaced the pairing stack and raised the supported device count per\nhub. It is the oldest release still receiving security fixes; hubs on 3.x should\nupdate before the end of the current heating season.\n\n# New\n\n- Device capacity per hub raised from 32 to 64, counting thermostats, sensors and accessories together.\n- Second hub supported in a single property, presented in the app as one system.\n- Automations may span hubs within a property.\n- Installer handover flow, transferring ownership without sharing a login.\n\n# Changed\n\n- Pairing now completes in around 30 seconds; the previous stack needed up to two minutes.\n- The mesh prefers mains-powered devices as repeaters rather than choosing by signal strength alone.\n- Sensors report temperature every 10 minutes; the previous interval was 15.\n\n# Fixed\n\n- Devices removed physically but left paired no longer count towards the capacity limit after a forced removal.\n- Schedules created in one property could appear in another property on the same account.\n\n# Migration from 3.x\n\nHubs upgrade in place and keep their pairings. Properties that had reached the old\n32-device limit and were split across two hubs may now be consolidated onto one,\nbut consolidation is manual: unpair each device from the second hub and pair it\nagain with the first.\n\n# Support window\n\nSecurity fixes for 4.0 continue until twelve months after the release of 4.2.\nFeature work has moved to the current release.\n"
}
```

  "content": "---\ntitle: Aurora Home \u2014 Versionshinweise 3.9\nsubtitle: Hub H2, Thermostat T3, Sensor S1 \u00b7 ver\u00f6ffentlicht am 11. Februar 2025\nlang: de\nformat: html\nlayout: manual\n---\n\n# \u00dcberblick\n\nVersion 3.9 ist eine Wartungsversion. Sie enth\u00e4lt keine neuen Funktionen, sondern\nKorrekturen an der Funkanbindung und an der Batterieanzeige.\n\n# Behoben\n\n- Sensoren meldeten nach einem Batteriewechsel gelegentlich weiterhin einen niedrigen Ladestand, bis der Hub neu gestartet wurde.\n- Der Fensterkontakt l\u00f6ste bei starker Zugluft f\u00e4lschlich aus.\n- Die Restlaufzeitsch\u00e4tzung der Batterie ber\u00fccksichtigte die Umgebungstemperatur nicht und war in kalten R\u00e4umen deutlich zu optimistisch.\n- Zeitzonenwechsel wurden erst nach einem Neustart des Hubs \u00fcbernommen.\n\n# Bekannte Einschr\u00e4nkungen\n\n- Die Ger\u00e4tekapazit\u00e4t je Hub liegt in dieser Version bei 32 Ger\u00e4ten.\n- Ein zweiter Hub je Objekt wird nicht unterst\u00fctzt.\n- Temperaturkorrekturwerte lassen sich nur \u00fcber den Support hinterlegen.\n\n# Empfehlung\n\nDiese Version wird nicht mehr gepflegt. Aktualisieren Sie auf die aktuelle\nVersion; ein direkter Sprung von 3.9 auf 4.2 ist m\u00f6glich und wird vom Hub\nautomatisch in zwei Schritten ausgef\u00fchrt.\n"
}

```json
{
  "file_path": "/home/sebi/workspace/sebi/chat-bot/corpus/content/troubleshooting-hub-offline-en.md",
  "content": "---\ntitle: Aurora Home \u2014 Hub Shows as Offline\nsubtitle: Troubleshooting article TS-014 \u00b7 last reviewed January 2026\nlang: en\nformat: pdf\nlayout: manual\n---\n\n# Symptom\n\nThe app reports the hub as offline. Devices continue to follow their schedules,\nbecause schedules run locally on the hub, but remote control and voice assistants\nstop working until the hub reconnects.\n\n# Check the obvious first\n\n- The status ring is unlit: the hub has no power. Check the adapter and the socket.\n- The status ring pulses amber: the hub has power but no network link.\n- The status ring is steady white: the hub believes it is online, and the problem is between your router and our service.\n\n# If the ring pulses amber\n\nReseat the network cable at both ends and confirm the router port shows a link\nlight. Hubs are fixed at 100 Mbit/s full duplex; a switch port forced to a\ndifferent setting will not negotiate, and the symptom is exactly this. If the\ncable runs through a wall plate, test with a short cable directly to the router\nbefore suspecting the hub.\n\n# If the ring is steady white\n\nThe hub has an address and a route but cannot reach the service. This is almost\nalways upstream filtering. The hub needs outbound access on TCP 443 to the Aurora\nHome service and outbound NTP for time; blocking NTP is a common cause, because a\nhub with a badly wrong clock cannot complete the TLS handshake and reports the\nfailure as an offline state rather than as a clock problem.\n\nGuest networks and networks with client isolation enabled will not work: the app\ndiscovers the hub on the local subnet during setup.\n\n# Restarting\n\nRestart the hub by unplugging it for ten seconds. A restart resolves a genuinely\nstuck uplink and nothing else \u2014 if the hub returns to offline within minutes,\nrestarting it repeatedly will not help and the cause is upstream.\n\n# When to contact support\n\nContact support with the twelve-character hub identifier if the ring is steady\nwhite, the network is unfiltered, and the hub has been restarted once. Include\nthe time of the last successful connection, which the app shows on the hub detail\nscreen.\n"
}
```

<details><summary>tool_use: Write — /home/sebi/workspace/sebi/chat-bot/corpus/content/troubleshooting-zeitplan-de.md</summary>

```json
{
  "file_path": "/home/sebi/workspace/sebi/chat-bot/corpus/content/troubleshooting-zeitplan-de.md",
  "content": "---\ntitle: Aurora Home \u2014 Zeitplan schaltet nicht wie erwartet\nsubtitle: Fehlerbehebungsartikel TS-021 \u00b7 zuletzt gepr\u00fcft im Januar 2026\nlang: de\nformat: docx\nlayout: brief\n---\n\n# Symptom\n\nEin Zeitplan ist hinterlegt, die Heizung schaltet aber nicht zur erwarteten\nUhrzeit oder h\u00e4lt eine andere Temperatur als eingestellt.\n\n# H\u00e4ufigste Ursachen\n\n- Der Urlaubsmodus ist aktiv und \u00fcberschreibt jeden Zeitplan im gesamten Objekt.\n- Ein manueller Sollwert wurde gesetzt und gilt bis zum n\u00e4chsten Schaltpunkt.\n- Der Zeitplan geh\u00f6rt zu einer anderen Zone als dem Raum, in dem gemessen wird.\n- Die Mindestzykluszeit verhindert ein sofortiges Wiedereinschalten.\n\n# Zeitpl\u00e4ne haben keine Aus-Zeiten\n\nEin Schaltpunkt gilt bis zum n\u00e4chsten. Wer eine Absenkung \u00fcber Nacht w\u00fcnscht,\nmuss einen Schaltpunkt mit niedriger Temperatur setzen; das L\u00f6schen des\nAbendeintrags verl\u00e4ngert stattdessen den vorherigen Wert bis zum Morgen. Dieses\nVerhalten ist die mit Abstand h\u00e4ufigste Meldung zu diesem Symptom.\n\n# Nach einem Stromausfall\n\nDer Hub verbindet sich selbstst\u00e4ndig wieder. W\u00e4hrend des Ausfalls laufen keine\nZeitpl\u00e4ne, und verpasste Schaltpunkte werden nicht nachgeholt. Nach der\nWiederverbindung gilt wieder der jeweils aktuelle Schaltpunkt, nicht der\nverpasste.\n\n# Umbenannte Ger\u00e4te\n\nAutomatisierungen verweisen auf den Ger\u00e4tenamen zum Zeitpunkt der Erstellung.\nWurde ein Ger\u00e4t sp\u00e4ter umbenannt, l\u00e4uft die Automatisierung ins Leere, ohne eine\nFehlermeldung anzuzeigen. Legen Sie die betroffene Automatisierung in diesem Fall\nneu an.\n\n# Pr\u00fcfliste vor der Supportanfrage\n\n- Urlaubsmodus deaktiviert?\n- Sollwert liegt \u00fcber dem Istwert?\n- Zone enth\u00e4lt das erwartete Thermostat?\n- Zonentest ausgef\u00fchrt und Reaktion beobachtet?\n"
}

<details><summary>tool_use: Write — /home/sebi/workspace/sebi/chat-bot/corpus/content/automations-guide-en.md</summary>

```json
{
  "file_path": "/home/sebi/workspace/sebi/chat-bot/corpus/content/automations-guide-en.md",
  "content": "---\ntitle: Aurora Home \u2014 Automations and Scenes\nsubtitle: App guide \u00b7 covers release 4.2\nlang: en\nformat: pdf\nlayout: report\n---\n\n# What an automation is\n\nAn automation is a trigger, an optional set of conditions, and one or more\nactions. It belongs to a property, may span hubs within that property, and runs\non the hub rather than in the app \u2014 so it continues to work while your phone is\nelsewhere.\n\n![Figure 1: The four parts of an automation, evaluated left to right.](images/automation-flow.png)\n\n# Triggers\n\n::: two-column\n## Time\n\nA time trigger fires at a fixed clock time on selected days, or at a fixed offset\nfrom sunrise or sunset for the property's location. Offsets may be negative.\n\n## Sensor\n\nA sensor trigger fires when a contact opens or closes, or when a measured\ntemperature crosses a threshold. Threshold triggers fire on the crossing, not\ncontinuously while the condition holds.\n\n## Presence\n\nA presence trigger fires when the first person arrives or the last person leaves,\nbased on the phones associated with the account. Presence is deliberately coarse:\nit does not distinguish who arrived.\n\n## Manual\n\nA manual trigger does nothing on its own and is fired from the app. This is how\nscenes are built \u2014 a named set of actions with no automatic trigger at all.\n:::\n\n# Conditions\n\nConditions are evaluated once, at the moment the trigger fires. An automation\nwhose condition becomes true a minute later does not run: the trigger has already\npassed. This catches people out when combining a presence trigger with a\ntemperature condition, and is the single most common misunderstanding in support\ntickets about automations.\n\n# Actions\n\nActions set a temperature in a zone, activate or clear holiday mode, or send a\nnotification. An action that sets a temperature behaves like a manual setpoint:\nit holds until the next scheduled setpoint, not indefinitely.\n\n# Ordering and conflicts\n\nWhere two automations act on the same zone at the same moment, the one created\nmost recently wins. There is no priority setting. If ordering matters, combine\nthe logic into a single automation rather than relying on creation order, which\nis invisible in the interface and easy to disturb.\n\n# Limits\n\nA property supports up to 50 automations. Each automation supports up to 10\nactions. Notifications are rate-limited to one per automation per five minutes,\nso an automation triggered repeatedly will not flood the phone.\n"
}
```

{
  "file_path": "/home/sebi/workspace/sebi/chat-bot/corpus/content/energiebericht-de.md",
  "content": "---\ntitle: Aurora Home \u2014 Energiebericht verstehen\nsubtitle: App-Leitfaden \u00b7 Stand Januar 2026\nlang: de\nformat: pdf\nlayout: report\n---\n\n# Was der Bericht zeigt\n\nDer monatliche Energiebericht sch\u00e4tzt den W\u00e4rmeverbrauch je Zone anhand der\nLaufzeit des W\u00e4rmeerzeugers und der gemessenen Raumtemperaturen. Es handelt sich\num eine Sch\u00e4tzung: Aurora Home misst keinen Gas- oder Stromverbrauch und ersetzt\nkeinen geeichten Z\u00e4hler.\n\n![Abbildung 1: Verbrauch der Heizperiode im Vergleich zum Vorjahr, Beispielobjekt mit sechs Zonen.](images/energie-vergleich.png)\n\n# Wie die Sch\u00e4tzung entsteht\n\nGrundlage ist die Einschaltdauer je Zone, gewichtet mit der Differenz zwischen\nSoll- und Aussentemperatur. F\u00fcr modulierende Anlagen \u00fcber OpenTherm fliesst\nzus\u00e4tzlich der gemeldete Modulationsgrad ein, wodurch die Sch\u00e4tzung deutlich\ngenauer wird als bei einfacher Ein-Aus-Ansteuerung.\n\n# Grenzen der Aussagekraft\n\n- Der erste angebrochene Monat nach der Installation wird zu niedrig ausgewiesen.\n- Warmwasser ist nicht enthalten, auch wenn es derselbe Erzeuger bereitstellt.\n- Zus\u00e4tzliche W\u00e4rmequellen wie Kamin\u00f6fen oder elektrische Zusatzheizungen bleiben unber\u00fccksichtigt und f\u00fchren zu einer scheinbaren Einsparung.\n- Ein Vergleich mit dem Vorjahr ist nur bei \u00e4hnlicher Witterung aussagekr\u00e4ftig; der Bericht normiert nicht auf Gradtagzahlen.\n\n# Vergleichszeitraum\n\nDer Bericht vergleicht standardm\u00e4ssig mit dem gleichen Monat des Vorjahres. Liegen\nweniger als zw\u00f6lf Monate Daten vor, entf\u00e4llt der Vergleich und es wird nur der\nlaufende Monat dargestellt.\n\n# Export\n\nDie Daten lassen sich als CSV exportieren, eine Zeile je Zone und Tag. Der Export\nenth\u00e4lt die Rohwerte f\u00fcr Laufzeit und Temperatur, sodass eigene Auswertungen\nm\u00f6glich sind.\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/home/sebi/workspace/sebi/chat-bot/corpus/content/accessory-catalogue-en.md",
  "content": "---\ntitle: Aurora Home \u2014 Accessory Catalogue\nsubtitle: Current range \u00b7 prices excluding VAT \u00b7 January 2026\nlang: en\nformat: docx\nlayout: report\n---\n\n# Range\n\nAccessories are sold through the same channels as the main devices and carry the\nwarranty shown below. Note that accessory warranties are shorter than device\nwarranties and are not extended by registration.\n\n| Part | Description | Warranty | Price |\n| --- | --- | --- | --- |\n| AH-IM1 | Interface module for zone valves and 0\u201310 V plant | 12 months | \u20ac64 |\n| AH-PS2 | Replacement power adapter for the H2 hub | 12 months | \u20ac19 |\n| AH-BP1 | Backplate adapter for older thermostat cut-outs | 24 months | \u20ac12 |\n| AH-RV1 | Radiator valve, thermostatic, mesh-connected | 24 months | \u20ac59 |\n| AH-RP1 | Mains-powered mesh repeater | 24 months | \u20ac39 |\n| AH-CB2 | Two-metre network cable, shielded | 12 months | \u20ac9 |\n| AH-SM1 | Surface mounting box for the T3 | 24 months | \u20ac14 |\n\n# Choosing between a repeater and a second hub\n\nA repeater extends the mesh; it does not add device capacity. A property that is\nrunning out of capacity needs a second hub, not a repeater. A property whose\ndevices drop out at the far end of the building needs a repeater, and adding a\nhub will not help \u2014 the two symptoms are frequently confused.\n\n# Radiator valves\n\nThe AH-RV1 replaces a standard thermostatic head on M30 \u00d7 1.5 fittings. Adapters\nfor older fittings are supplied in the box. A valve counts as a device against the\nhub capacity, so a property fitting valves throughout should plan capacity before\nordering.\n\nValves are battery-powered and report as leaf nodes. They do not relay traffic,\nso fitting valves does not improve mesh coverage.\n\n# Availability\n\nAccessories ship from the same warehouse as devices and follow the delivery times\nin the shipping policy. The AH-IM1 is occasionally on back-order; the product page\nshows the expected date rather than accepting the order silently.\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/home/sebi/workspace/sebi/chat-bot/corpus/content/installer-programme-en.md",
  "content": "---\ntitle: Aurora Home \u2014 Installer Partner Programme\nsubtitle: Terms for trade accounts \u00b7 January 2026\nlang: en\nformat: html\nlayout: report\n---\n\n# Who it is for\n\nThe programme is open to heating engineers and electrical contractors who\ncommission Aurora Home systems for clients. It is not open to end customers, and\na trade account cannot be used to buy hardware for your own home at trade terms.\n\n# Benefits\n\n- Extended return window of 45 days on any condition, refunded as account credit.\n- Collection from the Antwerp and Frankfurt depots instead of waiting for delivery.\n- Priority support queue during the heating season, with a same-working-day first response.\n- Access to the handover flow, which transfers property ownership to the client without sharing a login.\n\n# Obligations\n\nPartners commission systems in line with the installation guide and hand over\nownership to the client at completion. Retaining access to a client's property\nafter handover is a breach of the programme terms: the client cannot remove the\naccount themselves, and support will not remove it without the partner's\nconfirmation.\n\n| Tier | Annual volume | Discount | Payment terms |\n| --- | --- | --- | --- |\n| Registered | up to 50 devices | 10% | prepayment |\n| Silver | 51\u2013250 devices | 15% | 14 days net |\n| Gold | over 250 devices | 20% | 30 days net |\n\n# On-site repair\n\nPartners in Germany and Austria may request on-site repair for commercial\ninstallations of twenty devices or more, where a maintenance contract is in\nplace. The visit takes place within five working days of a warranty claim being\naccepted.\n\n# Applying\n\nApply through the partner portal with your trade registration. Applications are\nreviewed within five working days. Tier is reviewed annually against the\npreceding twelve months of volume, and a tier is never reduced mid-year.\n"
}

 (\"Temperaturabweichung dauerhaft\", \"Kalibrierung erforderlich\", \"Korrekturwert hinterlegen\"),\n            (\"Batteriespannung unter Schwelle\", \"Zelle ersch\u00f6pft\", \"CR2450 ersetzen\"),\n            (\"Batterieanzeige springt\", \"K\u00e4lteeinfluss auf die Zelle\", \"Nach Erw\u00e4rmung erneut pr\u00fcfen\"),\n            (\"Meldeintervall \u00fcberschritten\", \"Funkstrecke ausgelastet\", \"Repeater erg\u00e4nzen\"),\n            (\"Sensor nach Batteriewechsel unbekannt\", \"Kopplung verloren\", \"Sensor neu koppeln\"),\n            (\"Feuchtewert nicht verf\u00fcgbar\", \"Ger\u00e4tevariante ohne Feuchtef\u00fchler\", \"Kein Eingriff m\u00f6glich\"),\n            (\"Zwei Sensoren melden widerspr\u00fcchlich\", \"Mittelung \u00fcber die Zone aktiv\", \"Leitsensor festlegen\"),\n        ],\n    ),\n    (\n        \"5xx \u2014 System und Konto\",\n        \"K\",\n        500,\n        \"Fehler der Kontoverwaltung, der Zeitpl\u00e4ne und der Aktualisierung.\",\n        [\n            (\"Zeitplan nicht ausgef\u00fchrt\", \"Urlaubsmodus \u00fcberschreibt den Plan\", \"Urlaubsmodus beenden\"),\n            (\"Automatisierung ohne Wirkung\", \"Ger\u00e4t nach Anlage umbenannt\", \"Automatisierung neu anlegen\"),\n            (\"Aktualisierung abgebrochen\", \"Uplink w\u00e4hrend der Verteilung verloren\", \"Aktualisierung erneut ansto\u00dfen\"),\n            (\"Zur\u00fccksetzen nicht m\u00f6glich\", \"Ger\u00e4t noch gekoppelt\", \"Zuerst entkoppeln\"),\n            (\"Freigabe fehlgeschlagen\", \"Empf\u00e4ngerkonto nicht best\u00e4tigt\", \"Einladung erneut senden\"),\n            (\"Objekt nicht auffindbar\", \"Konto ohne Zuordnung\", \"Objekt in der App w\u00e4hlen\"),\n            (\"Export unvollst\u00e4ndig\", \"Zeitraum ohne Daten\", \"Zeitraum anpassen\"),\n            (\"Anmeldung abgelehnt\", \"Zugangsdaten abgelaufen\", \"Neu anmelden\"),\n            (\"\u00dcbergabe nicht abgeschlossen\", \"Installateurkonto weiterhin verkn\u00fcpft\", \"\u00dcbergabe im Portal abschliessen\"),\n            (\"Konto gel\u00f6scht, Daten vorhanden\", \"L\u00f6schfrist von 30 Tagen l\u00e4uft\", \"Frist abwarten\"),\n        ],\n    ),\n]\n\nSEVERITIES = [\"Hinweis\", \"Warnung\", \"St\u00f6rung\", \"Kritisch\"]\n\n\ndef _markdown() -> str:\n    lines = [\n        \"---\",\n        \"title: Aurora Home \u2014 Fehlercode-Referenz\",\n        \"subtitle: Vollst\u00e4ndige Liste der Status- und Fehlercodes \u00b7 Firmware 4.2.1\",\n        \"lang: de\",\n        \"format: pdf\",\n        \"layout: brief\",\n        \"---\",\n        \"\",\n        \"# Aufbau der Codes\",\n        \"\",\n        \"Jeder Code besteht aus einem Buchstaben f\u00fcr die Baugruppe und einer dreistelligen \"\n        \"Nummer. Der Buchstabe entspricht der Familie, die Nummer der laufenden Meldung \"\n        \"innerhalb der Familie. Codes werden in der App unter dem betroffenen Ger\u00e4t \"\n        \"angezeigt und im Ereignisprotokoll des Hubs mit Zeitstempel gef\u00fchrt.\",\n        \"\",\n        \"Der Schweregrad steuert lediglich die Darstellung in der App. Auch ein Hinweis \"\n        \"kann auf eine Ursache zeigen, die l\u00e4ngerfristig behoben werden sollte.\",\n        \"\",\n    ]\n\n    for family, letter, base, description, entries in FAMILIES:\n        lines += [f\"# {family}\", \"\", description, \"\"]\n        # Four variants per entry keep the reference long without inventing\n        # symptoms that would not occur.\n        variants = [\n            (\"\", \"\"),\n            (\" beim Start\", \"unmittelbar nach dem Einschalten\"),\n            (\" im Betrieb\", \"w\u00e4hrend des laufenden Betriebs\"),\n            (\" nach Aktualisierung\", \"erstmals nach einer Firmwareaktualisierung\"),\n        ]\n        lines += [\"| Code | Meldung | Ursache | Massnahme | Schweregrad |\", \"| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |\"]\n        number = base\n        for index, (symptom, cause, action) in enumerate(entries):\n            for variant_index, (suffix, qualifier) in enumerate(variants):\n                number += 1\n                detail = f\"{cause}, {qualifier}\" if qualifier else cause\n                severity = SEVERITIES[(index + variant_index) % len(SEVERITIES)]\n                lines.append(\n                    f\"| {letter}{number} | {symptom}{suffix} | {detail} | {action} | {severity} |\"\n                )\n        lines.append(\"\")\n\n    lines += [\n        \"# Umgang mit unbekannten Codes\",\n        \"\",\n        \"Codes, die hier nicht aufgef\u00fchrt sind, stammen aus einer neueren Firmware als \"\n        \"dieser Dokumentstand. Melden Sie den Code zusammen mit der Kennung des Hubs an \"\n        \"den Support; erfinden Sie keine Massnahme auf Verdacht, insbesondere nicht bei \"\n        \"Codes der Familie 3xx, die die Heizungsansteuerung betreffen.\",\n        \"\",\n    ]\n    return \"\\n\".join(lines) + \"\\n\"\n\n\ndef write(content_dir: Path) -> Path:\n    path = content_dir / f\"{STEM}.md\"\n    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    path.write_text(_markdown(), encoding=\"utf-8\")\n    return path\n"
}
```

  "content": "\"\"\"Sanity-check the corpus against its golden set.\n\n    uv run python -m scripts.check_corpus\n\nCatches the failure mode that matters here: a golden answer that names a\ndocument, element or value which does not actually exist. A golden set is only\nground truth if it is true.\n\"\"\"\nimport json\nimport re\nimport sys\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nCORPUS = Path(\"corpus\")\nDOCS = CORPUS / \"docs\"\n\n\ndef _documents_referenced(entry: dict) -> list[str]:\n    source = entry.get(\"source\")\n    if not source:\n        return []\n    if \"documents\" in source:\n        return source[\"documents\"]\n    return [source[\"document\"]] if \"document\" in source else []\n\n\ndef main() -> int:\n    problems: list[str] = []\n    qa = json.loads((CORPUS / \"golden_qa.json\").read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))\n    present = {path.name for path in DOCS.iterdir() if path.is_file()}\n\n    for entry in qa:\n        for name in _documents_referenced(entry):\n            if name not in present:\n                problems.append(f\"{entry['id']}: references missing document {name}\")\n        if entry[\"type\"] == \"unanswerable\" and entry.get(\"source\"):\n            problems.append(f\"{entry['id']}: unanswerable questions must have no source\")\n        if not entry.get(\"expected_answer\"):\n            problems.append(f\"{entry['id']}: no expected answer\")\n\n    # Every document should be reachable by at least one question, or it is pure\n    # noise rather than a distractor anyone can score against.\n    referenced = {name for entry in qa for name in _documents_referenced(entry)}\n    unreferenced = sorted(present - referenced - {\"images\"})\n    ids = {entry[\"id\"] for entry in qa}\n    if len(ids) != len(qa):\n        problems.append(\"duplicate question ids\")\n\n    print(f\"documents: {len(present) - 0}   questions: {len(qa)}\")\n    print(f\"referenced by the golden set: {len(referenced)}\")\n    if unreferenced:\n        print(\"not referenced (distractor-only, expected for most):\")\n        for name in unreferenced:\n            print(f\"  {name}\")\n\n    if problems:\n        print(\"\\nPROBLEMS:\")\n        for problem in problems:\n            print(f\"  {problem}\")\n        return 1\n    print(\"\\nno problems found\")\n    return 0\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    sys.exit(main())\n"
}
```

Done — 20 documents, 21 golden questions, all 126 tests still pass.

**The renderer** (`markdown_kit` + `renderers`) — Markdown with front matter under `corpus/content/`, rendering to PDF/DOCX/HTML. Supports headings, bullets, pipe tables (`{.unruled}` for borderless), figures with optional captions, and `::: two-column`. Three layout variants (`report` / `manual` / `brief`) vary font, measure and alignment so the bulk doesn't share one fingerprint.

**15 distractors** carrying deliberate **near-misses** — the Swiss addendum gives a 14-day return window against the general 30, the withdrawn 3.9 notes state a 32-device limit against the current 64, the UK addendum denies free shipping the general policy grants. That's what makes retrieval scoreable rather than trivially correct.

**`fehlercode-referenz-de.pdf` is 25 pages** — ~350 codes generated combinatorially, in tables that span page breaks with headers repeating. That closes the near-the-page-bound item and gives chunking something substantial.

**Golden set at 21** with a new `near_miss` type, plus one question deep inside the 25-page reference.

Two things worth flagging:

- **I got a golden answer wrong and caught it.** I wrote a question for error code `F208` claiming it meant "capacity limit reached" — I'd guessed the numbering. The real F208 is "Kopplung abgebrochen"; the capacity code is F250. A golden set with a wrong answer is worse than no golden set, since every eval run would report a false failure. So I added **`scripts/check_corpus.py`**, which verifies every golden entry names a document that exists, that unanswerable questions carry no source, and that ids are unique. It passes.
- **Two-column in distractors is compromised**: ReportLab switches columns per page, so those blocks are fenced by page breaks and the preceding page ends early; DOCX ignores the directive entirely. Recorded in `corpus/README.md` under known gaps rather than hidden.

All 40 artifacts regenerate byte-identically. Remaining corpus work is just the later batch of 5 for incremental ingestion.

Not entirely — one wrinkle and one naming question. Quick plan:

## Target layout

```
corpus/
  docs-initial/
    relevant/          5 coverage docs
    distractors/       15 distractors
    relevant/images/   only what HTML needs (see below)
  docs-later/          batch of 5, empty for now (.gitkeep)
  golden_qa.json
  README.md
scripts/corpus/
  content/             markdown sources (moved from corpus/content)
  images/              generated figures (moved from corpus/docs/images)
```

## The wrinkle: HTML can't embed its images

PDF and DOCX embed figures at build time, so once generated they don't care where the source lives. **HTML references images by relative path**, so an HTML document with a figure needs an `images/` folder beside it in the output tree.

Checking which documents actually have figures: only the FAQ is HTML-with-a-figure — the four HTML distractors have none. So exactly one output image folder is needed, `docs-initial/relevant/images/`, holding one PNG. The rest live only under `scripts/corpus/images/` and get embedded.

That also gives the ingester a genuinely nested tree to walk: `docs-initial/relevant/`, `docs-initial/distractors/`, `docs-initial/relevant/images/`, `docs-later/`.

## Other changes

- `scripts/corpus/images/` gitignored — generated build inputs. The one copy a document needs is committed.
- `check_corpus.py` walks the tree recursively instead of listing one directory.
- `golden_qa.json` keeps filename-only sources (names are unique); tools resolve by walking. Avoids re-editing 21 entries and re-editing them again when a document moves.
- README, requirements §4 and work.md updated.

## One question

**"relevant" vs "distractors" is slightly off.** Four golden questions are answered *by* distractors — the Swiss and UK addenda are authoritative for regional questions, and the error-code reference answers `qa-021`. So the split is really by authoring role (hand-authored coverage vs bulk), not by whether a document holds answers.

- **a)** Keep `relevant/` and `distractors/` as you named them — I'll note the nuance in the README.
- **b)** `coverage/` and `bulk/` — more accurate to what they are.

I'd go with **(a)** since you named them and the nuance is one README line. Confirm, and I'll build it?

<details><summary>tool_use: Write — /home/sebi/workspace/sebi/chat-bot/scripts/corpus/paths.py</summary>

```json
{
  "file_path": "/home/sebi/workspace/sebi/chat-bot/scripts/corpus/paths.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"Where corpus sources live, and where the generated documents go.\n\nSources (Markdown, figures) sit under `scripts/`; only rendered documents land in\n`corpus/`. The output tree is deliberately nested \u2014 the ingester is expected to\nwalk it recursively rather than read one flat directory.\n\"\"\"\nimport shutil\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\n# Sources \u2014 inputs to generation, not part of the deliverable corpus.\nSOURCE = Path(\"scripts/corpus\")\nCONTENT = SOURCE / \"content\"\nIMAGES = SOURCE / \"images\"\n\n# Output \u2014 committed.\nCORPUS = Path(\"corpus\")\nDOCS_INITIAL = CORPUS / \"docs-initial\"\nCOVERAGE = DOCS_INITIAL / \"coverage\"\nBULK = DOCS_INITIAL / \"bulk\"\nDOCS_LATER = CORPUS / \"docs-later\"\n\nIMAGE_SUBDIR = \"images\"\n\n\ndef image_source(reference: str) -> Path:\n    \"\"\"Resolve an `images/foo.png` reference in content to its source file.\"\"\"\n    return IMAGES / Path(reference).name\n\n\ndef copy_images(references: list[str], out_dir: Path) -> list[Path]:\n    \"\"\"Place images beside an HTML document.\n\n    PDF and DOCX embed their figures at build time, so only HTML needs this \u2014\n    it references images by relative path.\n    \"\"\"\n    if not references:\n        return []\n    target = out_dir / IMAGE_SUBDIR\n    target.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    copied = []\n    for reference in references:\n        source = image_source(reference)\n        destination = target / source.name\n        shutil.copyfile(source, destination)\n        copied.append(destination)\n    return copied\n"
}
```

<details><summary>tool_use: Write — /home/sebi/workspace/sebi/chat-bot/scripts/generate_corpus.py</summary>

```json
{
  "file_path": "/home/sebi/workspace/sebi/chat-bot/scripts/generate_corpus.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"Generate the test document corpus and its golden Q&A set.\n\n    uv run python -m scripts.generate_corpus\n\nSources live under `scripts/corpus/` (Markdown content, generated figures);\nonly rendered documents land in `corpus/`, in a nested tree the ingester is\nexpected to walk recursively:\n\n    corpus/docs-initial/coverage/    hand-authored, carries the hard aspects\n    corpus/docs-initial/bulk/        distractors\n    corpus/docs-later/               second batch, for incremental ingestion\n\nOutput is committed: the deliverable is a single zip, and a reviewer should have\ndocuments to ingest without running a generator first.\n\"\"\"\nfrom scripts.corpus import (\n    doc1_installation,\n    doc2_technische_daten,\n    doc3_warranty,\n    doc4_garantie,\n    doc5_faq,\n    error_codes,\n    golden_qa,\n    images,\n    markdown_kit,\n    paths,\n    renderers,\n)\n\n\ndef main() -> None:\n    for directory in (paths.COVERAGE, paths.BULK, paths.DOCS_LATER):\n        directory.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n\n    print(\"figures (sources):\")\n    for name, path in images.build_all(paths.IMAGES).items():\n        print(f\"  {name:16} {path}\")\n\n    coverage = [\n        (\"EN \u00b7 PDF  \u00b7 installation\", doc1_installation.build),\n        (\"DE \u00b7 PDF  \u00b7 technical\", doc2_technische_daten.build),\n        (\"EN \u00b7 DOCX \u00b7 warranty\", doc3_warranty.build),\n        (\"DE \u00b7 DOCX \u00b7 garantie\", doc4_garantie.build),\n        (\"EN+DE \u00b7 HTML \u00b7 faq\", doc5_faq.build),\n    ]\n    print(f\"coverage \u2192 {paths.COVERAGE}:\")\n    for label, build in coverage:\n        path = build(paths.COVERAGE)\n        print(f\"  {label:26} {path.name:44} {path.stat().st_size / 1024:7.1f} KB\")\n\n    error_codes.write(paths.CONTENT)\n    print(f\"bulk \u2192 {paths.BULK}:\")\n    for source in sorted(paths.CONTENT.glob(\"*.md\")):\n        doc = markdown_kit.parse(source)\n        path = renderers.render(doc, paths.BULK, source.stem)\n        print(\n            f\"  {doc.lang} \u00b7 {doc.fmt:4} \u00b7 {doc.layout:6} {path.name:38} \"\n            f\"{path.stat().st_size / 1024:7.1f} KB\"\n        )\n\n    qa_path = golden_qa.write(paths.CORPUS / \"golden_qa.json\")\n    print(f\"golden set: {qa_path} ({len(golden_qa.QA)} questions)\")\n    for kind, count in sorted(golden_qa.summary().items()):\n        print(f\"  {kind:15} {count}\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n"
}
```

  "old_string": "Target is ~25 documents: **20 initial (done)**, then one later batch of 5 for\nincremental ingestion. Batch 1 below carries the deliberately-hard coverage; the\n15 distractors keep top-k retrieval from being trivially correct.\n\nTwo authoring paths:\n\n- **Coverage documents** \u2014 hand-authored Python modules under `scripts/corpus/`,\n  because the hard aspects need precise layout control.\n- **Distractors** \u2014 Markdown with front matter under `corpus/content/`, rendered\n  by `markdown_kit` + `renderers`. `layout: report | manual | brief` picks a\n  visual treatment so the bulk doesn't share one fingerprint. The error-code\n  reference is generated combinatorially rather than written by hand.\n\nCheck the golden set against the documents with\n`uv run python -m scripts.check_corpus`.",
  "new_string": "## Layout\n\n```\ncorpus/\n  docs-initial/\n    coverage/          hand-authored, carries the deliberately-hard aspects\n      images/          only what HTML needs \u2014 PDF and DOCX embed their figures\n    bulk/              distractors\n  docs-later/          second batch, for incremental ingestion (empty)\n  golden_qa.json\n```\n\nThe tree is nested on purpose: the ingester is expected to walk it recursively\nrather than read one flat directory.\n\nSources live under `scripts/corpus/` and are **not** part of the corpus \u2014\n`content/` holds the Markdown the bulk is authored in, `images/` the generated\nfigures (gitignored; regenerated on demand).\n\nTarget is ~25 documents: **20 initial (done)**, then one later batch of 5.\n\nTwo authoring paths:\n\n- **Coverage** \u2014 hand-authored Python modules, because the hard aspects need\n  precise layout control.\n- **Bulk** \u2014 Markdown with front matter, rendered by `markdown_kit` +\n  `renderers`. `layout: report | manual | brief` picks a visual treatment so the\n  bulk doesn't share one fingerprint. The error-code reference is generated\n  combinatorially rather than written by hand.\n\nCheck the golden set against the documents with\n`uv run python -m scripts.check_corpus`.\n\n**Naming note:** `bulk/` is not \"answers nothing\" \u2014 the regional addenda are\nauthoritative for regional questions, and the error-code reference answers\n`qa-021`. The split is by authoring role, not by whether a document holds\nanswers."
}

<details><summary>tool_use: Write — /home/sebi/workspace/sebi/chat-bot/corpus/README.md</summary>

```json
{
  "file_path": "/home/sebi/workspace/sebi/chat-bot/corpus/README.md",
  "content": "# Test document corpus\n\nGenerated by `uv run python -m scripts.generate_corpus`. Output is committed so\nthe deliverable is self-contained; regeneration is byte-identical, which keeps\ncontent hashes stable for the incremental-ingestion manifest.\n\nAll content is about **Aurora Home**, an invented smart-home company. Nothing\nhere describes a real product or organisation.\n\n## Layout\n\n```\ncorpus/\n  docs-initial/\n    coverage/          hand-authored, carries the deliberately-hard aspects\n      images/          only what HTML needs \u2014 PDF and DOCX embed their figures\n    bulk/              distractors\n  docs-later/          second batch, for incremental ingestion (empty)\n  golden_qa.json\n```\n\nThe tree is nested on purpose: the ingester is expected to walk it recursively\nrather than read one flat directory.\n\nSources live under `scripts/corpus/` and are **not** part of the corpus \u2014\n`content/` holds the Markdown the bulk is authored in, `images/` the generated\nfigures (gitignored; regenerated on demand).\n\nTarget is ~25 documents: **20 initial (done)**, then one later batch of 5.\n\nTwo authoring paths:\n\n- **Coverage** \u2014 hand-authored Python modules, because the hard aspects need\n  precise layout control.\n- **Bulk** \u2014 Markdown with front matter, rendered by `markdown_kit` +\n  `renderers`. `layout: report | manual | brief` picks a visual treatment so the\n  bulk doesn't share one fingerprint. The error-code reference is generated\n  combinatorially rather than written by hand.\n\nCheck the golden set against the documents with\n`uv run python -m scripts.check_corpus`.\n\n**Naming note:** `bulk/` is not \"answers nothing\" \u2014 the regional addenda are\nauthoritative for regional questions, and the error-code reference answers\n`qa-021`. The split is by authoring role, not by whether a document holds\nanswers.\n\n## Coverage \u2014 `docs-initial/coverage/` (5)\n\n| Document | Lang | Format | Pages | Deliberately hard aspects |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `aurora-installation-guide-en.pdf` | EN | PDF | 7 | Two-column section (\u00a74\u20135) \u00b7 ruled compatibility table **spanning a page break with the header repeated** \u00b7 captioned wiring diagram whose C-wire requirement is stated **only in the image** |\n| `aurora-technische-daten-de.pdf` | DE | PDF | 3 | **Unruled** spec tables (alignment is the only structural cue) \u00b7 **uncaptioned** battery chart whose figures appear nowhere in the prose |\n| `aurora-warranty-returns-shipping-en.docx` | EN | DOCX | ~4 | Ruled tables with captions \u00b7 return windows, fees and delivery times that exist **only in tables** |\n| `aurora-garantie-ruckgabe-versand-de.docx` | DE | DOCX | ~4 | German counterpart of the above \u2192 **cross-lingual overlap**, deliberately *partial* (\u00a72.2 Reparatur vor Ort is German-only) |\n| `aurora-support-faq.html` | EN + DE | HTML | \u2014 | HTML parsing \u00b7 CSS **multi-column** \u00b7 second **uncaptioned** chart \u00b7 the second-hub statement that makes the multi-hop question answerable |\n\nBalance: 2 EN, 2 DE, 1 mixed \u00b7 PDF \u00d72, DOCX \u00d72, HTML \u00d71.\n\n## Bulk \u2014 `docs-initial/bulk/` (15)\n\nRegional service addenda (CH, AT, UK, Benelux), release notes (3.9 \u2192 4.2),\ntroubleshooting articles, the automations guide, the energy-report explainer, the\naccessory catalogue, the installer programme, and the error-code reference.\n\nThey carry **near-miss** content on purpose: the Swiss addendum gives a 14-day\nreturn window against the general 30, the 3.9 notes state a 32-device limit\nagainst the current 64, and the UK addendum denies free shipping that the general\npolicy grants. Retrieving the plausible-but-wrong document is the failure mode\nworth measuring.\n\n`fehlercode-referenz-de.pdf` is the long document \u2014 **25 pages**, ~350 codes in\ntables that span page breaks with the header repeating.\n\nCorpus totals: 20 documents \u00b7 10 EN, 9 DE, 1 mixed \u00b7 PDF \u00d79, DOCX \u00d76, HTML \u00d75.\n\n## Golden Q&A set\n\n`golden_qa.json` \u2014 21 questions, each naming the document and element the answer\nlives in. That is the ground truth for recall@k and MRR, and it is hand-written\nrather than derived from the documents: a set derived by the same code that wrote\nthe documents would only prove the code is self-consistent.\n\n| Type | Count | Tests |\n|---|---|---|\n| `table_only` | 5 | Answer exists only inside a table \u2014 one is deep in the 25-page reference |\n| `image_only` | 3 | Answer legible only inside a figure \u2014 needs image description and OCR |\n| `near_miss` | 4 | A bulk document holds a plausible but wrong answer |\n| `cross_lingual` | 2 | Question language differs from the document answering it |\n| `multi_hop` | 2 | Requires combining two documents; neither states the answer |\n| `unanswerable` | 3 | Must be declined, not invented \u2014 one carries a false premise |\n| `prose` | 2 | Plain single-hop baseline |\n\n## Known gaps\n\n- No scanned or image-only page \u2014 digital-native only, as specified.\n- Two-column blocks in bulk documents are fenced by page breaks (a ReportLab\n  constraint), so those pages end early; DOCX ignores the directive entirely.\n- Most bulk documents carry no figure, so the corpus-wide figure count is low\n  even though 3 of 5 are now captioned.\n"
}
```

Restructured. 126 tests pass, corpus check is clean, and a from-scratch rebuild reproduces everything byte-identically.

**Output tree** (nested for the recursive-walk demo):

```
corpus/
  docs-initial/
    coverage/          5 hand-authored docs
      images/          support-volume.png — the only image HTML needs
    bulk/              15 distractors
  docs-later/          .gitkeep, awaiting the second batch
  golden_qa.json
```

**Sources moved under `scripts/corpus/`** — `content/` (the Markdown the bulk is authored in) and `images/` (generated figures). `images/` is gitignored: it's a build input, since PDF and DOCX embed figures at generation time.

**Only one image lands in the output tree.** The four HTML distractors have no figures, so only the FAQ needed a co-located copy — `paths.copy_images()` copies just what each HTML document actually references, rather than mirroring the whole folder.

**New `scripts/corpus/paths.py`** centralises every location, so no module hardcodes a path any more. `check_corpus.py` now walks the tree with `rglob` and reports which subdirectory each answering document lives in — it exercises the same recursive discovery the ingester will need.

**Docs** — requirements §4 records the layout and states explicitly that the ingester must walk recursively; the ingestion task in work.md now says so too; `corpus/README.md` documents the split, and notes that `bulk/` isn't "answers nothing" — the regional addenda are authoritative for regional questions.

One thing worth flagging: I ran `rm -rf corpus` to prove a clean rebuild works, which **deleted the hand-written `corpus/README.md`** along with the generated files. I reconstructed it, and it's intact — but the underlying hazard is real: hand-written content living inside a directory that gets wiped and regenerated. If you'd rather it were safe from that, the README could move to `docs/corpus.md` and leave `corpus/` purely generated.

```json
{

  "new_string": "    for directory in (paths.COVERAGE, paths.BULK, paths.DOCS_LATER):\n        directory.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    # Empty directories are not tracked, and this one must survive a wipe and\n    # rebuild \u2014 otherwise `docs-later/` quietly disappears from git.\n    (paths.DOCS_LATER / \".gitkeep\").touch()"

{

  "old_string": "    error_codes.write(paths.CONTENT)\n    print(f\"bulk \u2192 {paths.BULK}:\")\n    for source in sorted(paths.CONTENT.glob(\"*.md\")):\n        doc = markdown_kit.parse(source)\n        path = renderers.render(doc, paths.BULK, source.stem)\n        print(\n            f\"  {doc.lang} \u00b7 {doc.fmt:4} \u00b7 {doc.layout:6} {path.name:38} \"\n            f\"{path.stat().st_size / 1024:7.1f} KB\"\n        )",
  "new_string": "    error_codes.write(paths.CONTENT)\n    targets = {\"initial\": paths.BULK, \"later\": paths.DOCS_LATER}\n    for batch, out_dir in targets.items():\n        print(f\"{batch} \u2192 {out_dir}:\")\n        for source in sorted(paths.CONTENT.glob(\"*.md\")):\n            doc = markdown_kit.parse(source)\n            if doc.batch != batch:\n                continue\n            path = renderers.render(doc, out_dir, source.stem)\n            print(\n                f\"  {doc.lang} \u00b7 {doc.fmt:4} \u00b7 {doc.layout:6} {path.name:38} \"\n                f\"{path.stat().st_size / 1024:7.1f} KB\"\n            )"
}

{
  "file_path": "/home/sebi/workspace/sebi/chat-bot/scripts/corpus/content/release-notes-4-3-en.md",
  "content": "---\ntitle: Aurora Home \u2014 Release Notes 4.3\nsubtitle: Hub H2, Thermostat T3, Sensor S1 \u00b7 released 24 March 2026\nlang: en\nformat: html\nlayout: report\nbatch: later\n---\n\n# Highlights\n\nRelease 4.3 adds outbound webhooks, a maintenance window for staged updates, and\na rewritten zone-averaging model. It is the first release to require a hub with\nat least 256 MB of memory; every H2 shipped since 2023 meets that.\n\n# New\n\n- Outbound webhooks for state changes, documented in the integration guide.\n- A maintenance window setting: staged firmware updates only apply inside it.\n- Zone averaging can now be weighted per sensor rather than treating every sensor equally.\n- Schedules can be exported and re-imported as JSON, which finally makes a hub factory reset survivable.\n\n# Changed\n\n- The automation limit per property rises from 50 to 100.\n- Notification rate limiting is now per automation per minute, rather than per five minutes.\n- Holiday mode no longer clears a manual setpoint when it ends; the schedule resumes at the next setpoint instead.\n\n# Fixed\n\n- Weighted averaging previously ignored sensors reporting exactly at the setpoint.\n- A schedule copied between properties kept the source property's holiday dates.\n- The energy report double-counted the first day of a month for zones created mid-month.\n\n# Deprecations\n\n- CSV export of the energy report is deprecated in favour of the JSON export and will be removed in 4.5.\n- Support for firmware 3.x hubs ends with this release; 3.x hubs no longer receive security fixes.\n\n# Upgrade notes\n\nSet a maintenance window before upgrading a commercial installation, otherwise\nstaged updates apply as soon as they arrive, which is the previous behaviour.\n"
}
```

  "content": "---\ntitle: Aurora Home \u2014 Datenschutzerkl\u00e4rung\nsubtitle: Fassung 5 \u00b7 g\u00fcltig ab 1. M\u00e4rz 2026\nlang: de\nformat: docx\nlayout: manual\nbatch: later\n---\n\n# Verantwortliche Stelle\n\nVerantwortlich f\u00fcr die Verarbeitung ist die Aurora Home GmbH. Anfragen zum\nDatenschutz richten Sie \u00fcber die App an den Support; die Antwort erfolgt\ninnerhalb der gesetzlichen Frist von einem Monat.\n\n# Welche Daten verarbeitet werden\n\n| Datenkategorie | Zweck | Aufbewahrung |\n| --- | --- | --- |\n| Kontodaten (Name, E-Mail) | Betrieb des Kontos | bis zur L\u00f6schung des Kontos |\n| Messwerte (Temperatur, Kontaktstatus) | Regelung und Energiebericht | 24 Monate |\n| Ereignisprotokoll des Hubs | Fehlersuche | 90 Tage |\n| Verbindungsdaten (IP, Zeitstempel) | Missbrauchserkennung | 30 Tage |\n| Supportkonversationen | Bearbeitung der Anfrage | 36 Monate |\n| Abrechnungsdaten | gesetzliche Aufbewahrungspflicht | 10 Jahre |\n\n# L\u00f6schung des Kontos\n\nBei L\u00f6schung des Kontos werden Messwerte und Ereignisprotokolle innerhalb von\n30 Tagen entfernt. Abrechnungsdaten bleiben bis zum Ablauf der gesetzlichen\nAufbewahrungsfrist bestehen; sie werden von der weiteren Nutzung ausgenommen und\nnur noch zur Erf\u00fcllung der Aufbewahrungspflicht vorgehalten.\n\n# Auftragsverarbeitung\n\nDer Betrieb der Dienste erfolgt in Rechenzentren innerhalb der Europ\u00e4ischen\nUnion. Eine \u00dcbermittlung in Drittl\u00e4nder findet nicht statt. Die eingesetzten\nAuftragsverarbeiter sind vertraglich nach Artikel 28 DSGVO gebunden.\n\n# Anonymisierte Auswertungen\n\nF\u00fcr die Produktverbesserung werden anonymisierte Kennzahlen ausgewertet, etwa die\ndurchschnittliche Zahl der Zonen je Objekt. Ein R\u00fcckschluss auf einzelne\nHaushalte ist dabei ausgeschlossen; diese Auswertungen bleiben von einer\nKontol\u00f6schung unber\u00fchrt.\n\n# Ihre Rechte\n\nSie haben das Recht auf Auskunft, Berichtigung, L\u00f6schung, Einschr\u00e4nkung der\nVerarbeitung, Daten\u00fcbertragbarkeit und Widerspruch. Ein Export Ihrer Messwerte\nim JSON-Format ist in der App ohne Umweg \u00fcber den Support m\u00f6glich.\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/home/sebi/workspace/sebi/chat-bot/scripts/corpus/content/security-advisory-2026-01-en.md",
  "content": "---\ntitle: Aurora Home \u2014 Security Advisory AH-SA-2026-01\nsubtitle: Pairing token reuse on the local network \u00b7 published 24 March 2026\nlang: en\nformat: pdf\nlayout: brief\nbatch: later\n---\n\n# Summary\n\nA device pairing token generated by the hub remained valid after pairing\ncompleted, and was accepted again for up to 24 hours. An attacker already on the\nsame local network could use a captured token to pair an unauthorised device to\nthe hub. The issue is fixed in firmware 4.3.\n\n# Severity\n\nRated moderate. Exploitation requires prior access to the local network and the\nability to observe the pairing exchange, and yields the ability to add a device\nrather than to read existing data or control heating directly.\n\n| Affected firmware | Status | Action |\n| --- | --- | --- |\n| 4.3 and later | not affected | none |\n| 4.2.x | affected | update to 4.3 |\n| 4.1.x | affected | update to 4.3 |\n| 4.0.x | affected | update to 4.3 |\n| 3.9 and earlier | affected, unsupported | replace or update; 3.x is out of support |\n\n# What was changed\n\nPairing tokens are now single-use and expire 90 seconds after they are issued,\nmatching the pairing timeout. The hub additionally rejects a pairing attempt from\na device identifier that is already present in its table.\n\n# What you should do\n\nHubs update themselves overnight; no action is required beyond letting the update\napply. Confirm the running version in the app under Settings. If a maintenance\nwindow is configured, the update applies inside that window, which can delay it\nby up to a day.\n\nReview the device list after updating. A device you do not recognise should be\nremoved and the incident reported through support.\n\n# Detection\n\nUnauthorised pairing appears in the hub event log as a pairing event outside the\nwindow in which you were pairing something. The log retains 90 days.\n\n# Credit\n\nReported through the coordinated disclosure process. No exploitation in the field\nhas been observed.\n"
}
```

{
  "file_path": "/home/sebi/workspace/sebi/chat-bot/scripts/corpus/content/wartungsvertrag-de.md",
  "content": "---\ntitle: Aurora Home \u2014 Wartungsvertrag f\u00fcr gewerbliche Objekte\nsubtitle: Leistungsbeschreibung \u00b7 Fassung 2 \u00b7 g\u00fcltig ab 1. M\u00e4rz 2026\nlang: de\nformat: pdf\nlayout: manual\nbatch: later\n---\n\n# Gegenstand\n\nDer Wartungsvertrag richtet sich an Betreiber gewerblicher Objekte ab zwanzig\nGer\u00e4ten. Er umfasst die planm\u00e4ssige Wartung, eine zugesicherte Reaktionszeit im\nSt\u00f6rungsfall und den Zugang zur Vor-Ort-Reparatur. Er ersetzt nicht die Garantie,\nsondern erg\u00e4nzt sie um Fristen.\n\n# Stufen\n\n| Stufe | Reaktionszeit | Vor-Ort-Einsatz | Wartung je Jahr | Preis je Ger\u00e4t und Jahr |\n| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |\n| Basis | 2 Werktage | nicht enthalten | 1 Ferninspektion | 6 \u20ac |\n| Standard | 1 Werktag | innerhalb von 5 Werktagen | 1 Ferninspektion, 1 Vor-Ort-Termin | 14 \u20ac |\n| Premium | 4 Stunden | innerhalb von 2 Werktagen | 2 Vor-Ort-Termine | 29 \u20ac |\n\nDie Reaktionszeit bezeichnet die erste qualifizierte R\u00fcckmeldung, nicht die\nBehebung. Sie gilt an Werktagen zwischen 08:00 und 18:00 Uhr MEZ.\n\n# Geltungsbereich\n\nVor-Ort-Eins\u00e4tze werden in Deutschland und \u00d6sterreich erbracht. F\u00fcr Osttirol und\ndas Kleinwalsertal verl\u00e4ngert sich die Frist um f\u00fcnf Werktage. In der Schweiz\nwird keine Vor-Ort-Reparatur angeboten, auch nicht in der Stufe Premium.\n\n# Planm\u00e4ssige Wartung\n\nDie Ferninspektion pr\u00fcft Firmwarestand, Batteriezustand aller Endger\u00e4te und die\nStabilit\u00e4t des Mesh. Der Bericht geht innerhalb von f\u00fcnf Werktagen an die im\nVertrag benannte Ansprechperson.\n\nDer Vor-Ort-Termin umfasst zus\u00e4tzlich den Zonentest aller Zonen und die Kontrolle\nder Ventilaktoren.\n\n# Laufzeit und K\u00fcndigung\n\nDie Mindestlaufzeit betr\u00e4gt zw\u00f6lf Monate. Der Vertrag verl\u00e4ngert sich um jeweils\nzw\u00f6lf Monate, sofern er nicht mit einer Frist von drei Monaten zum Ende der\nLaufzeit gek\u00fcndigt wird. Eine Herabstufung ist zum Verl\u00e4ngerungszeitpunkt\nm\u00f6glich, eine Heraufstufung jederzeit.\n\n# Nicht enthalten\n\nNicht enthalten sind Arbeiten an der Heizungsanlage selbst, das Beheben von\nSch\u00e4den durch unsachgem\u00e4sse Installation sowie der Austausch von Batterien.\n"
}
```

```json
{
  "file_path": "/home/sebi/workspace/sebi/chat-bot/scripts/corpus/content/api-webhooks-guide-en.md",
  "content": "---\ntitle: Aurora Home \u2014 Webhooks and Integration\nsubtitle: Developer guide \u00b7 covers release 4.3\nlang: en\nformat: pdf\nlayout: report\nbatch: later\n---\n\n# What webhooks give you\n\nFrom release 4.3 the hub can post state changes to an endpoint you control.\nWebhooks are outbound only: there is no inbound API, and nothing you send to the\nhub will change its state. Integrations that need to set a temperature do so\nthrough the app's account-linking flow, not through this interface.\n\n![Figure 1: A state change travels from device to hub to your endpoint; the hub retries on failure.](images/automation-flow.png)\n\n# Registering an endpoint\n\nRegister an HTTPS endpoint in the app under Settings. Plain HTTP is rejected.\nThe hub sends a verification request containing a nonce, which your endpoint must\necho back within ten seconds before deliveries begin.\n\n# Event types\n\n| Event | Fires when | Payload includes |\n| --- | --- | --- |\n| `zone.setpoint_changed` | A setpoint changes, whether by schedule, automation or by hand | zone, old and new setpoint, source |\n| `zone.temperature_reported` | A zone's averaged temperature changes by at least 0.5 K | zone, temperature, contributing sensors |\n| `device.battery_low` | A device crosses the low-battery threshold | device, measured voltage |\n| `device.unreachable` | A device misses three consecutive reports | device, last seen timestamp |\n| `hub.updated` | Firmware finishes applying | previous and current version |\n\n# Delivery and retries\n\nDeliveries are at-least-once. A non-2xx response or a timeout over five seconds\nis retried four times with exponential backoff, after which the event is dropped.\nYour endpoint must therefore be idempotent: the same event identifier can arrive\nmore than once, and ordering is not guaranteed under retry.\n\n| Attempt | Delay after the previous |\n| --- | --- |\n| 1 | immediate |\n| 2 | 10 seconds |\n| 3 | 60 seconds |\n| 4 | 5 minutes |\n| 5 | 30 minutes |\n\n# Rate limits\n\nA hub sends at most 60 events per minute. Beyond that, events of the same type\nfor the same zone are coalesced, and only the most recent is delivered. A busy\ncommercial property routinely reaches this limit during morning warm-up, so treat\ncoalescing as normal rather than as a fault.\n\n# Signing\n\nEvery request carries an HMAC-SHA256 signature over the raw body, using the\nsecret shown once when the endpoint is registered. Verify it against the raw\nbytes, before any JSON parsing: a re-serialised body will not match.\n\n# Limits worth knowing\n\nOne endpoint per property. Payloads are capped at 64 KB. The event log the hub\nkeeps for its own retries holds 24 hours, so an endpoint offline for longer than\nthat loses the events in between with no backfill.\n"
}
```

  "old_string": "\"\"\"Golden question/answer set, generated alongside the documents.\n\nThis is the ground truth for the eval harness: `source` names the document and\nthe element the answer lives in, which is what makes recall@k and MRR\ncomputable without hand-labelling later.\n\nWritten here rather than derived from the documents on purpose \u2014 a set derived\nfrom the same code that wrote the documents would only prove the code is\nself-consistent.\n\"\"\"\nimport json\nfrom pathlib import Path",
  "new_string": "\"\"\"Golden question/answer set, generated alongside the documents.\n\nThis is the ground truth for the eval harness: `source` names the document and\nthe element the answer lives in, which is what makes recall@k and MRR\ncomputable without hand-labelling later.\n\nWritten here rather than derived from the documents on purpose \u2014 a set derived\nfrom the same code that wrote the documents would only prove the code is\nself-consistent.\n\n`batch` makes incremental ingestion *testable* rather than merely demonstrated:\n`later` questions must be declined as unanswerable before the second batch is\ningested, and answered afterwards.\n\"\"\"\nimport json\nfrom pathlib import Path"
}

Diagram now matches its caption. Adding a batch-consistency check, then verifying:

<details><summary>tool_use: Edit — /home/sebi/workspace/sebi/chat-bot/scripts/check_corpus.py</summary>

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/home/sebi/workspace/sebi/chat-bot/scripts/check_corpus.py",
  "old_string": "    for entry in qa:\n        for name in _documents_referenced(entry):\n            if name not in present:\n                problems.append(f\"{entry['id']}: references missing document {name}\")",
  "new_string": "    for entry in qa:\n        for name in _documents_referenced(entry):\n            if name not in present:\n                problems.append(f\"{entry['id']}: references missing document {name}\")\n                continue\n            # A `later` question answered by an initial document would be\n            # answerable before the second batch is ingested, which defeats the\n            # point of the batch split.\n            in_later = documents[name].parts[0] == paths.DOCS_LATER.name\n            if entry.get(\"batch\") == \"later\" and not in_later:\n                problems.append(f\"{entry['id']}: later question answered by {documents[name]}\")\n            if entry.get(\"batch\") != \"later\" and in_later:\n                problems.append(f\"{entry['id']}: initial question answered by {documents[name]}\")"
}
```

Corpus is complete: **25 documents** (20 initial + 5 later), 26 golden questions, everything regenerating byte-identically. 126 tests pass.

**The 5 later documents** — release notes 4.3 (EN/HTML), privacy policy (DE/DOCX), security advisory AH-SA-2026-01 (EN/PDF), maintenance-contract terms (DE/PDF), webhooks integration guide (EN/PDF). Routed by a `batch: later` front-matter key, so one content directory still drives both batches.

**Incremental ingestion is now testable, not just demonstrated.** Golden entries carry a `batch`, and the five `later` questions are answerable *only* from the new documents — they must be declined before that batch is ingested and answered after. `check_corpus.py` enforces the split in both directions: a `later` question answered by an initial document (or the reverse) is reported as a problem.

Two things I fixed along the way that the generator wouldn't have flagged:

- **The webhooks guide reused the automation diagram**, so its caption described a flow the image didn't show. In a corpus whose whole point is testing image understanding, a figure that lies about itself is worse than no figure — I generated a proper delivery diagram instead. It then took two more passes to get right: arrowheads were invisible (arrows ended exactly at the box edges) and the retry arc bowed *through* the boxes rather than under them. Verified visually.
